- dataviz: Use this skill whenever you are about to create ANY chart, graph, plot, dashboard, or data visualization, in ANY output medium — an HTML or React artifact, inline SVG, plotting code in any library (matplotlib, plotly, d3, Recharts, …), an image/PNG you will render and upload, or a chart shared into Slack. Read it BEFORE writing the first line of chart code, choosing chart colors, building a stat tile / meter / KPI row, or laying out a dashboard. Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. Teaches a design-system-agnostic method: a form heuristic, a color formula with a runnable validator, mark specs, and interaction rules. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. Triggers on: "chart", "graph", "plot", "data viz", "visualization", "dashboard", "analytics", "visualize data", "categorical colors", "sequential / diverging palette", "stat tile", "sparkline", "heatmap", "legend", "axis", "tooltip", "chart colors", "color by series".
- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors ("from now on when X", "each time X", "whenever X", "before/after X") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions ("allow X", "add permission", "move permission to"), env vars ("set X=Y"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: "allow npm commands", "add bq permission to global settings", "move permission to user settings", "set DEBUG=true", "when claude stops show X". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.
- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: "rebind ctrl+s", "add a chord shortcut", "change the submit key", "customize keybindings".
- simplify: Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.
- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.
- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo, defaults to 10m) - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. "check the deploy every 5 minutes", "keep running /babysit-prs"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.
- claude-api: Reference for the Claude API / Anthropic SDK — model ids, pricing, params, streaming, tool use, MCP, agents, caching, token counting, model migration.
TRIGGER — read BEFORE opening the target file; don't skip because it "looks like a one-liner" — whenever: the prompt names Claude/Anthropic in any form (Claude, Anthropic, Fable, Opus, Sonnet, Haiku, `anthropic`, `@anthropic-ai`, `claude-*`, `us.anthropic.*`, `[1m]`); the user asks about an LLM (pricing/model choice/limits/caching) — never answer from memory; OR the task is LLM-shaped with provider unstated (agent/MCP/tool-definition/multi-agent/RAG/LLM-judge/computer-use; generate/summarize/extract/classify/rewrite/converse over NL; debugging refusals/cutoffs/streaming/tool-calls/tokens).
SKIP only when another provider is being worked on (overrides all triggers): OpenAI/GPT/Gemini/Llama/Mistral/Cohere/Ollama named in the query; OR `grep -rE 'openai|langchain_openai|google.generativeai|genai|mistralai|cohere|ollama'` over the project hits (run this grep FIRST if no provider named — don't Read the file).
- run: Launch and drive this project's app to see a change working. Use when asked to run, start, or screenshot the app, or to confirm a change works in the real app (not just tests). First looks for a project skill that already covers launching the app; otherwise falls back to built-in patterns per project type (CLI, server, TUI, Electron, browser-driven, library).
- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation
- review: Review a GitHub pull request; for your working diff use /code-review
- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch

127	  19: { wave: 19, ids: [345, 346, 344, 343] },
128	  20: { wave: -1, ids: [345, 346, 344] },
129	};
130	
131	export const WAVE_ANNOUNCE: Record<number, Record<number, WaveAnnounce>> = {
132	  [MOON_KIND_FROST]: FROST_ANNOUNCE,
133	  [MOON_KIND_PUMPKIN]: PUMPKIN_ANNOUNCE,
134	};
135	
136	/** Lang.GetInvasionWaveText（Lang.cs:1131-1153）：Game.FinalWave(-1)/FirstWave(1)/Wave(n)
137	 *  + Game.InvasionWave_TypeN（N=id 数，1..6 个怪名） */
138	export function invasionWaveText(a: WaveAnnounce): string {
139	  const label = a.wave < 0 ? Lang.text('Game.FinalWave')
140	    : a.wave === 1 ? Lang.text('Game.FirstWave')
141	      : Lang.text('Game.Wave', a.wave);
142	  const names = a.ids.map((id) => Lang.npcName(id) ?? String(id));
143	  return Lang.text(`Game.InvasionWave_Type${a.ids.length}`, label, ...names);
144	}
145	
146	/** startPumpkinMoon / startSnowMoon（Main.cs:10903-10940）：
147	 *  置位 + 清另一事件 + 清血月 + wave=1 + 广播 wave 1 文案。
148	 *  调用方须先过门（夜晚 && 无进行中事件）。返回 wave 1 公告（已激活则 null） */
149	export function startMoonEvent(w: World, kind: 1 | 2): WaveAnnounce | null {
150	  const me = w.moonEvent;
151	  if (me.kind !== 0) return null;
152	  me.kind = kind;
153	  me.totalInvasionPoints = 0;
154	  me.waveKills = 0;
155	  me.waveNumber = 1;
156	  w.clock.bloodMoon = false;   // Main.cs:10905-10906 / 10925-10926
157	  return WAVE_ANNOUNCE[kind][1];
158	}
159	
160	/** stopMoonEvent（Main.cs:10879-10901）：清事件 + 三字段归零（单机 netMode!=1 分支） */
161	export function stopMoonEvent(w: World): void {
162	  const me = w.moonEvent;
163	  if (me.kind === 0) return;
164	  me.kind = 0;
165	  me.totalInvasionPoints = 0;
166	  me.waveKills = 0;
167	  me.waveNumber = 0;
168	}
169	
170	/** GetMoonEventPointScalar（NPC.cs:79230-79241）：大师 ×2.5 / 专家 ×2 / 普通 ×1 */
171	function pointScalar(expert: boolean, master: boolean): number {
172	  if (master) return 2.5;
173	  if (expert) return 2;
174	  return 1;
175	}
176	
177	/**
178	 * CheckProgressFrostMoon / CheckProgressPumpkinMoon（NPC.cs:79245-79372 / 79487-79513）。
179	 * 击杀计分：0 分怪直接 return；得分累入 waveKills 与 totalInvasionPoints；
180	 * waveKills ≥ lookup[wave] 且 lookup[wave]≠0 → waveKills 清零（**溢出分数不结转**）、
181	 * waveNumber+1，返回新波公告（wave 20 后 lookup=0 不再进波 → null）。
182	 * 必须在掉落结算之后同帧调用（原版 checkDead：NPCLoot → CheckProgress，NPC.cs:79020-79021），
183	 * 保证本波掉落用的是进波前的 wave。
184	 */
185	export function addMoonEventKill(
186	  w: World, vanillaId: number, expert = false, master = false,
187	): WaveAnnounce | null {
188	  const me = w.moonEvent;
189	  if (me.kind === 0) return null;
190	  const pts = POINTS_BY_NPC[vanillaId];
191	  if (!pts) return null;   // 未列出 = 0 分，原版 num2 恒 0 → 不动进度也不刷进度条
192	  const need = REQUIRED_POINTS[me.waveNumber] ?? 0;
193	  // 公告文案在进波判定【之前】按旧波号取（switch 用的 waveNumber 是旧值）
194	  const announce = WAVE_ANNOUNCE[me.kind]?.[me.waveNumber + 1] ?? null;
195	  const gained = pts * pointScalar(expert, master);
196	  me.waveKills += gained;
197	  me.totalInvasionPoints += gained;
198	  if (me.waveKills >= need && need !== 0) {
199	    me.waveKills = 0;
200	    me.waveNumber++;
201	    return announce;
202	  }
203	  return null;
204	}
205	

```

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-13T05:40:03.552Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
Drops conditions are implemented. One thing to check: the vanilla `PumpkinMoonDropGatingChance` uses `(24 - num) / 2.5` and TS uses `Math.trunc(((frost ? 28 : 24) - wave) / 2.5)` ✓. And `int` cast in C# truncates toward zero; for positive values, same as Math.trunc. But if `(24-wave)` is negative (wave 24+... impossible since wave≤20; with expert +5, wave could be 20+5=25 → 24-25 = -1 → -1/2.5 = -0.4 → C# (int)(-0.4) = 0 → then num2<1 → 1. TS Math.trunc(-0.4) = -0 → -0 < 1 → 1. Same. ✓

The `FrostMoonDropGateForTrophies` implementation: `let denom = me.wave >= 19 ? 2 : me.wave >= 17 ? 3 : 4;` — vanilla: default 4; 16→4; 17→3; 18→3; 19→2; >=20→2. TS: wave 15/16→4, 17/18→3, 19+/20→2 ✓.

Expert branch: `Main.expertMode && Main.rand.Next(3) == 0` → num--. TS: `ctx.isExpert && ctx.rng.int(0,2)===0` ✓ (Next(3) = 0..2).

Then `info.rng.Next(num) == 0` → TS `ctx.rng.int(0, Math.max(1,denom)-1) === 0` ✓.

Note: masterMode does NOT count for these (vanilla checks Main.expertMode only). TS uses ctx.isExpert ✓.

OK, drops are in place. Now let me verify where moonEvent ctx is injected at the kill site (Game.ts:8877-8878), and check `counts`/`bossSlotSum` construction.
```

</details>

135	  int(min: number, max: number): number {

// 原版文案查询门面。1:1 对照 Terarria1456/Terraria/Lang.cs 的便捷方法,
// 核心在 LanguageManager.ts(对照 LanguageManager.cs)。
//
// 数据 = public/l10n/<culture>.json(scripts/build-l10n.mjs 构建,扁平
// { [category]: { [key]: value } },全键 = category + '.' + key,已做英文兜底 overlay)。
// 例:Lang.text('UI.Play')、Lang.text('ItemName.HealingPotion')、Lang.text('LegacyWorldGen.9')
import { languageManager } from './LanguageManager';
import { ITEM_KEY_TO_ID, ITEM_NAME_BY_ID, NPC_NAME_BY_ID, BUFF_NAME_BY_ID, PROJECTILE_NAME_BY_ID, TILE_NAME_BY_ID, TILE_NAME_ITEM_BY_SHEET, TILE_NAME_ZH_BY_ID, TILE_NAME_EN_BY_ID, ITEM_NAME_ZH_BY_ID, ITEM_NAME_EN_BY_ID, WALL_NAME_ITEM_BY_WALL, WALL_NAME_ZH_BY_ID, WALL_NAME_EN_BY_ID } from './idNames.generated';
import { ITEM_BY_KEY, ITEM_DEFS } from '../data/items';
import { TILE_BY_KEY, TILE_DEFS } from '../data/tiles';
import { worldgenProgressKey, worldgenProgressStringKey } from './worldgenKeys';

export const Lang = {
  get loaded(): boolean { return languageManager.loaded; },
  /** 语言列表(设置面板数据源,来自 l10n/index.json) */
  get cultures() { return languageManager.cultures; },
  get activeCultureName(): string | null { return languageManager.activeCulture?.name ?? null; },
  get onChange() { return (fn: () => void) => languageManager.onChange(fn); },

  /** 启动初始化(默认 zh-Hans);load() 为旧签名兼容 */
  init(lang?: string | number): Promise<boolean> { return languageManager.init(lang); },
  async load(): Promise<boolean> { return Lang.init(); },

  /** 切换语言并广播(onLanguageChanged);失败返回 false 不改变状态 */
  setLanguage(culture: string | number): Promise<boolean> { return languageManager.setLanguage(culture); },

  /** 点路径取文案(全键 = category.key)。支持 {0}/{1} 占位符。缺失回退 key 本身并 warn 一次 */
  text(path: string, ...args: (string | number)[]): string {
    return languageManager.getTextValue(path, ...args);
  },

  /** Main.ValueToCoins（Main.cs:54649-54689 1:1）：铜币总值 →
   *  "N 铂金 N 金 N 银 N 铜"（非零档空格拼接，Currency.* 文案 toLowerCase，
   *  去尾空格；0 → 空串）。死亡掉钱行（Game.DroppedCoins {0}）与价格显示用 */
  valueToCoins(value: number): string {
    let rest = Math.max(0, Math.floor(value));
    let plat = 0, gold = 0, silver = 0;
    while (rest >= 1000000) { rest -= 1000000; plat++; }
    while (rest >= 10000) { rest -= 10000; gold++; }
    while (rest >= 100) { rest -= 100; silver++; }
    const copper = rest;
    let text = '';
    if (plat > 0) text += `${plat} ${Lang.text('Currency.Platinum').toLowerCase()} `;
    if (gold > 0) text += `${gold} ${Lang.text('Currency.Gold').toLowerCase()} `;
    if (silver > 0) text += `${silver} ${Lang.text('Currency.Silver').toLowerCase()} `;
    if (copper > 0) text += `${copper} ${Lang.text('Currency.Copper').toLowerCase()} `;
    if (text.length > 0) text = text.slice(0, -1);
    return text;
  },

  has(path: string): boolean { return languageManager.exists(path); },

  /** 聊天池随机一条（XxxChatter 类目；原版 Language.SelectRandom(Lang.CreateDialogFilter)） */
  chatter(category: string): string | null { return languageManager.randomFromCategory(category); },

  /** CreateDialogFilter 前缀随机（NPC.HasSpecialEventText 的 "XxxSpecialText.Yyy" 族；
   *  原版 Language.SelectRandom(Lang.CreateDialogFilter(prefix))，Lang.cs:425-428） */
  specialText(prefix: string): string | null { return languageManager.randomFromPrefix(prefix); },

  // ---- Legacy 数组等价物(Lang.cs:475-551 InitializeLegacyLocalization)----

```

// 死亡掉钱文案 ValueToCoins（Main.cs:54649-54689 1:1）
import { describe, it, expect, beforeAll } from 'vitest';
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { Lang } from '../src/i18n/Lang';
import { languageManager } from '../src/i18n/LanguageManager';

describe('Lang.valueToCoins（Main.ValueToCoins 1:1）', () => {
  beforeAll(() => {
    // 真实 zh-Hans 包注入（fetch 在 node 测试不可用——同 random-text.test 模式）
    const pack = JSON.parse(readFileSync('public/l10n/zh-Hans.json', 'utf8'));
    languageManager.loadPackJson(pack);
  });

  it('逐级拆解：白金×1M/金×10K/银×100/铜', () => {
    expect(Lang.valueToCoins(0)).toBe('');
    expect(Lang.valueToCoins(50)).toBe('50 铜');
    expect(Lang.valueToCoins(12345)).toBe('1 金 23 银 45 铜');
    expect(Lang.valueToCoins(100)).toBe('1 银');
    expect(Lang.valueToCoins(10000)).toBe('1 金');
    expect(Lang.valueToCoins(1000000)).toBe('1 铂金');
    // 12,345,678 = 12 铂金 + 34,5678? 验算：12,345,678 - 12×1,000,000 = 345,678
    // 345,678 → 34 金（340,000）余 5,678 → 56 银（5,600）余 78 铜
    expect(Lang.valueToCoins(12345678)).toBe('12 铂金 34 金 56 银 78 铜');
  });

  it('只显示非零档（原版 if(numN>0) 链）', () => {
    expect(Lang.valueToCoins(10100)).toBe('1 金 1 银');          // 0 铜不显示
    expect(Lang.valueToCoins(1000001)).toBe('1 铂金 1 铜');      // 金银为 0 跳过
  });

  it('死亡行文案拼装（Game.DroppedCoins {0}）', () => {
    expect(Lang.text('Game.DroppedCoins', Lang.valueToCoins(12345)))
      .toBe(`掉了${Lang.valueToCoins(12345)}`);
    expect(Lang.text('Game.DroppedCoins', Lang.valueToCoins(12345))).toContain('1 金');
  });
});

```

20	function b64ToBytes(b64: string): Uint8Array {
21	  const s = atob(b64);
22	  const u8 = new Uint8Array(s.length);
23	  for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) u8[i] = s.charCodeAt(i);
24	  return u8;
25	}
26	
27	function readVarint(u8: Uint8Array, pos: { i: number }): number {
28	  let v = 0, shift = 0;
29	  while (pos.i < u8.length) {
30	    const b = u8[pos.i++];
31	    v |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
32	    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
33	    shift += 7;
34	  }
35	  return v;
36	}
37

163	  // 物品 id 还原(箱子/背包/储物就地重写):v3+ 走稳定 id;v2 旧档直读(接受破损政策)。
164	  // v4 起 armor/dye/trash/misc/loadouts 也是稳定 id;v3 恰为裸内部下标——用迁移锚
165	  // 快照(LEGACY_KEY_OF_INTERNAL_V3)反查 key 再转 vi_/原定义(本地物品退役兼容层)
166	  const v3 = (data.version ?? 1) >= 3;
167	  const v4 = (data.version ?? 1) >= 4;
168	  const mapItem = (it: SavedItemT | null): SavedItemT | null =>
169	    it && v3 ? { ...it, id: it.id === 0 ? 0 : ITEM_INTERNAL_OF_STABLE.get(it.id) ?? 0 } : it;
170	  const mapLegacySlot = (it: SavedItemT | null): SavedItemT | null => {
171	    if (!it) return it;
172	    if (v4) return mapItem(it);
173	    if (!v3) return it; // v2 及更早:裸下标直读
174	    // v3:armor/dye/trash/misc/loadouts 段 = 保存时构建的裸内部下标
175	    const key = LEGACY_KEY_OF_INTERNAL_V3[it.id];
176	    if (key === undefined) return it;
177	    if (/^vi_(\d+)_/.test(key)) {
178	      // vi_ 键:键本身仍在注册表(蛇形/驼峰双注册内容等价),直接反查内部 id。
179	      // ★ 不能经 ITEM_STABLE_OF_INTERNAL[裸下标] —— 本地 184 条删除后下标整体左移
180	      const id = ITEM_BY_KEY[key];
181	      return id !== undefined ? { ...it, id } : it;
182	    }
183	    // 本地键:退役映射(→vi_);纯自创删除键→归 0
184	    const viId = viIdOfRetired(key);
185	    return { ...it, id: viId > 0 ? viId : 0 };
186	  };
187	  // v3 稳定 id → 内部 id(含 locked 透传);★曾未接线直接用 data.chests——
188	  // 现有物品 stable≡internal 掩盖,自定义物品进箱即错
189	  const v3Chests = v3
190	    ? data.chests.map((c) => ({ x: c.x, y: c.y, locked: c.locked, items: c.items.map(mapItem) }))
191	    : data.chests;
192	  if (v3 && data.player) {
193	    data.player = {
194	      ...data.player,
195	      inventory: data.player.inventory.map(mapItem),
196	      banks: data.player.banks?.map((b) => b.map(mapItem)),
197	      armor: data.player.armor?.map(mapLegacySlot),
198	      dye: data.player.dye?.map(mapLegacySlot),
199	      trash: mapLegacySlot(data.player.trash ?? null),
200	      miscEquips: data.player.miscEquips?.map(mapLegacySlot),
201	      miscDyes: data.player.miscDyes?.map(mapLegacySlot),
202	      loadouts: data.player.loadouts?.map((l) => ({
203	        armor: l.armor.map(mapLegacySlot),
204	        dye: l.dye.map(mapLegacySlot),
205	      })),
206	    };
207	  }
208	  const world = new WorldClass(data.header.width, data.header.height, data.header.seed, data.header.name);
209	  world.spawnX = data.header.spawn[0];
210	  world.spawnY = data.header.spawn[1];
211	  world.groundLevel = data.header.groundLevel;
212	  world.rockLevel = data.header.rockLevel;
213	  // 旧档缺 lavaLine（原版 UnderworldLayer = maxTilesY-200）
214	  world.lavaLine = data.header.lavaLine ?? data.header.height - 200;
215	  if (data.header.treeX) world.treeX = data.header.treeX;
216	  if (data.header.treeStyle) world.treeStyle = data.header.treeStyle;
217	  if (data.header.treeTops?.length) world.treeTops = data.header.treeTops;
218	  if (data.header.crimson !== undefined) world.crimson = data.header.crimson;
219	  if (data.header.dungeonX !== undefined) world.dungeonX = data.header.dungeonX;
220	  // dungeonY 缺省（旧档/导入）回退地表线：CheckToSpawnDungeonEnemies 的 pY<dungeonY+40 门槛近似成立
221	  if (data.header.dungeonY !== undefined && data.header.dungeonY > 0) world.dungeonY = data.header.dungeonY;
222	  else world.dungeonY = world.groundLevel;
223	  if (data.header.jungleX !== undefined) world.jungleX = data.header.jungleX;
224	  world.clock.timeOfDay = data.clock.timeOfDay;
225	  world.clock.dayCount = data.clock.dayCount;
226	  // 原版 header _temp 三件套（WF:1276-1278）;旧档缺省=无血月/无日食/满月
227	  if (data.clock.bloodMoon !== undefined) world.clock.bloodMoon = data.clock.bloodMoon;
228	  if (data.clock.eclipse !== undefined) world.clock.eclipse = data.clock.eclipse;
229	  if (data.clock.moonPhase !== undefined) world.clock.moonPhase = data.clock.moonPhase;
230	  if (data.header.moonType !== undefined) world.moonType = data.header.moonType;
231	  if (data.header.seedFlags) world.seedFlags = { ...data.header.seedFlags };
232	  // 世界难度（Main.GameMode，Main.cs:2677）——旧档缺省 0=经典
233	  if (data.header.difficulty !== undefined) world.difficulty = data.header.difficulty;
234	  // 旅程时间倍率/冻结（CreativePowers.cs:866/:1448）——旧档缺省 1/false
235	  if (data.header.journeyTimeRate !== undefined) world.journeyTimeRate = data.header.journeyTimeRate;
236	  if (data.header.journeyFreezeTime !== undefined) world.journeyFreezeTime = data.header.journeyFreezeTime;
237	  // 日晷/月晷四件套（WorldFile.cs:1311/1342/1416-1417；旧档缺省=无快进无冷却）
238	  if (data.header.sundialCooldown !== undefined) world.sundialCooldown = data.header.sundialCooldown;
239	  if (data.header.moondialCooldown !== undefined) world.moondialCooldown = data.header.moondialCooldown;
240	  if (data.header.fastForwardTimeToDawn !== undefined) world.fastForwardTimeToDawn = data.header.fastForwardTimeToDawn;
241	  if (data.header.fastForwardTimeToDusk !== undefined) world.fastForwardTimeToDusk = data.header.fastForwardTimeToDusk;
242	  if (data.invasion) { // 入侵五元组回填（旧档缺省=无入侵）
243	    world.invasionType = data.invasion.type;
244	    world.invasionSize = data.invasion.size;
245	    world.invasionSizeStart = data.invasion.sizeStart;
246	    world.invasionX = data.invasion.x;
247	    world.invasionDelay = data.invasion.delay;
248	  }

```

---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n/** 全 miss 桩：next()=0.5 → 任意 N(n≥2) 恒不命中；int(lo,hi) 恒取 lo */\nfunction allMissDeps(over: Partial<MoonSpawnDeps> = {}): MoonSpawnDeps {\n  return {\n    count: () => 0,\n    any: () => false,\n    bossCap: false,\n    next: () => 0.5,\n    int: (lo) => lo,\n    ...over,\n  };\n}\n\n/** 顺序命中桩：values 依次供 next()（耗尽后回退  ...over,\n  });\n}\n\n/** 击杀 n 只 netId（返回最后一条进波公告，无进波返回 null） */\nfunction kills(w: World, id: number, n: number, expert = false, master = false) {\n  let ann: ReturnType<typeof addMoonEventKill> = null;\n  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) ann = addMoonEventKill(w, id, expert, master);\n  return ann;\n}\n\nfunction moonWorld(kind: 1 | 2) {\n  const w = new World(32, 32, 7, 'moon-test');\n  startMoonEvent(w, kind);\n  return w;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// ① 分数表 / 分值表 / 入侵组号（数据表逐值转录）\n// 1（:91-125）', () => {\n    // rng.int(0,denom-1)=0 恒命中（桩 int 取 lo）→ 只验公式放行的 denom\n    expect(evalCond('PumpkinMoonDropGatingChance', [], dropCtx({}))).toBe(true);\n    // 普通 wave15 → ⌊(24-15)/2.5⌋=3；专家 wave15 → ⌊(24-20)/2.5⌋=1，再 -1 → 钳 1\n    const expert = dropCtx({ isExpert: true });\n    expect(expert.moonEvent).toMatchObject({ kind: 2, wave: 15 });\n    expect(evalCond('PumpkinMoonDropGatingChance', [], expert)).toBe(true);\n    // 无事件 / 霜月期间 → false\n    expect(evalCond('PumpkinMoonDropGatingChance', [], dropCtx({ moonEvent: undefined }))).toBe(false);\n    expect(evalCond('PumpkinMoonDropGatingChance', [], dropCtx({ moonEvent: { kind: 1, wave: 15 } }))).toBe(false);\n  });\n\n  it('FrostMoonDropGatingChance：基数 28，专家 denom-2（:55-89）', () => {\n    const frost = dropCtx({ moonEvent: { kind: 1, wave: 15 } });\n    expect(evalCond('FrostMoonDropGatingChance', [], frost)).toBe(true);\n    expect(evalCond('FrostMoonDropGatingChance', [], dropCtx({ moonEvent: { kind: 2, wave: 15 } }))).toBe(false);\n    expect(evalCond('FrostMoonDropGatingChance', [], dropCtx({ moonEvent: undefined }))).toBe(false);\n  });\n\n  it('掷骰语义：RollLuck(denom)==0（denom>1 时未命中 0 → 不放行）', () => {\n    // 桩 int 恒返回 lo=1 → Next(denom)=1 ≠ 0 → 不放行（denom≥2 时）\n    const missRng = { next: () => 0, int: () => 1 -1（:127-229）', () => {\n    // int(lo)=0 → Next(denom)=0 放行；wave 15/16→4、17/18→3、19/20→2\n    for (const [wv, denom] of [[15, 4], [16, 4], [17, 3], [18, 3], [19, 2], [20, 2]] as const) {\n      const c = dropCtx({ moonEvent: { kind: 1, wave: wv } });\n      expect(evalCond('FrostMoonDropGateForTrophies', [], c), `wave ${wv}`).toBe(true);\n      // denom=1 时（专家 -1 后）恒放行；否则未掷中 0 → false\n      const moonEvent: { kind: 2, wave: 19 } }))).toBe(true);\n    expect(evalCond('PumpkinMoonDropGateForTrophies', [], dropCtx({ moonEvent: { kind: 1, wave: 19 } }))).toBe(false);\n  });\n});\n\n// 193	 * 击杀计分：0 分怪直接 return；得分累入 waveKills 与 totalInvasionPoints；
194	 * waveKills ≥ lookup[wave] 且 lookup[wave]≠0 → waveKills 清零（**溢出分数不结转**）、
195	 * waveNumber+1，返回新波公告（wave 20 后 lookup=0 不再进波 → null）。
196	 * 必须在掉落结算之后同帧调用（原版 checkDead：NPCLoot → CheckProgress，NPC.cs:79020-79021），
197	 * 保证本波掉落用的是进波前的 wave。
198	 */
199	export function addMoonEventKill(
200	  w: World, vanillaId: number, expert = false, master = false,
201	): WaveAnnounce | null {
202	  const me = w.moonEvent;
203	  if (me.kind === 0) return null;
204	  const pts = POINTS_BY_NPC[vanillaId];
205	  if (!pts) return null;   // 未列出 = 0 分，原版 num2 恒 0 → 不动进度也不刷进度条
206	  const need = REQUIRED_POINTS[me.waveNumber] ?? 0;
207	  // 公告文案在进波判定【之前】按旧波号取（switch 用的 waveNumber 是旧值）
208	  const announce = WAVE_ANNOUNCE[me.kind]?.[me.waveNumber + 1] ?? null;
209	  const gained = pts * pointScalar(expert, master);
210	  me.waveKills += gained;
211	  me.totalInvasionPoints += gained;
212	  if (me.waveKills >= need && need !== 0) {
213	    me.waveKills = 0;
214	    me.waveNumber++;
215	    return announce;
216	  }
217	  return null;
218	}
219

 "new_string": "  it('南瓜月分值表（NPC.cs:79455-79488）：稻草人族 1/无头骑士 50/哀木 75/南瓜王 150', () => {\n    expect(PUMPKIN_POINTS_BY_NPC[305]).toBe(1);\n    for (const id of [306, 307, 308, 309, 310, 311, 312, 313, 314]) {\n      expect(PUMPKIN_POINTS_BY_NPC[id], `id ${id}`).toBe(1);\n    }\n    expect(PUMPKIN_POINTS_BY_NPC[315]).toBe(50);   // 无头骑士\n    expect(PUMPKIN_POINTS_BY_NPC[325]).toBe(75);   // 哀木\n    expect(PUMPKIN_POINTS_BY_NPC[326]).toBe(2);    // 树精\n    expect(PUMPKIN_POINTS_BY_NPC[327]).toBe(150);  // 南瓜王\n    expect(PUMPKIN_POINTS_BY_NPC[329]).toBe(5);    // 地狱犬\n    expect(PUMPKIN_POINTS_BY_NPC[330]).toBe(10);   // 胡闹鬼\n    expect(Object.keys(PUMPKIN_POINTS_BY_NPC)).toHaveLength(16);\n  });\n\n  it('霜月分值表（NPC.cs:79312-79354）：冰女王 150/圣诞坦克 100/常绿尖叫怪 50', () => {\n    expect([FROST_POINTS_BY_NPC[338], FROST_POINTS_BY_NPC[339], FROST_POINTS_BY_NPC[340]])\n      .toEqual([1, 1, 1]);                          // 精灵僵尸三型\n    expect(FROST_POINTS_BY_NPC[341]).toBe(20);      // 礼物宝箱怪\n    expect(FROST_POINTS_BY_NPC[342]).toBe(2);       // 姜饼人\n    expect(FROST_POINTS_BY_NPC[343]).toBe(18);      // 雪怪\n    expect(FROST_POINTS_BY_NPC[344]).toBe(50);      // 常绿尖叫怪（圣诞树）\n    expect(FROST_POINTS_BY_NPC[345]).toBe(150);     // 冰雪女王\n    expect(FROST_POINTS_BY_NPC[346]).toBe(100);     // 圣诞坦克\n    expect(FROST_POINTS_BY_NPC[347]).toBe(8);       // 精灵直升机\n    expect([FROST_POINTS_BY_NPC[348], FROST_POINTS_BY_NPC[349]]).toEqual([4, 4]); // 胡桃夹士两型\n    expect(FROST_POINTS_BY_NPC[350]).toBe(3);       // 精灵弓箭手\n    expect(FROST_POINTS_BY_NPC[351]).toBe(10);      // 坎卜斯\n    expect(FROST_POINTS_BY_NPC[352]).toBe(5);       // 弗洛科\n    expect(Object.keys(FROST_POINTS_BY_NPC)).toHaveLength(15);\n  });"
